            '<div class="tb-act-main" style="width:' + mainW + 'px;background-color:' + color + ';color:' + text +
            (wip && wip.bold ? ';font-weight:bold' : '') + '">' +
            '<span class="tb-act-label">' + esc(label) + '</span>' + badge +
            (canEdit() && mainW >= 24 ? '<i class="tb-h tb-h-l"></i><i class="tb-h tb-h-r"></i>' : '') + '</div>';
        if (monW > 0) {
            h += '<div class="tb-tail tb-tail-mon" style="left:' + mainW + 'px;width:' + monW + 'px;background-color:' + color + '" title="Monitor ' + hours(a.monitor_hours) + '"></div>';
        }
        if (pilW > 0) {
            h += '<div class="tb-tail tb-tail-pil" style="left:' + (mainW + monW) + 'px;width:' + pilW + 'px;background-color:' + color + '" title="Pilot ' + hours(a.pilot_hours) + '"></div>';
        }
        if (eds) {
            h += '<span class="tb-lock-tag">🔒 ' + esc($.map(eds, function (x2) { return x2.name + (x2.mode === 'drag' ? ' 拖曳中' : ' 編輯中'); }).join('、')) + '</span>';
        }
        return h + '</div>';
    }

    function tooltip(a) {
        var e = ms(a.end_at), lines = [
            a.tool_id + '  ' + catLabel(a.category) + (a.pm_type ? '（' + a.pm_type + '）' : ''),
            'WIP：' + wipLabel(a.wip_mode),
            '時間：' + short(ms(a.start_at)) + ' ～ ' + short(e)
        ];
        if (a.need_monitor) {
            lines.push('Monitor ' + hours(a.monitor_hours) + '：至 ' + short(e + a.monitor_hours * HOUR));
        }
        if (a.need_pilot) {
            lines.push('Pilot ' + hours(a.pilot_hours) + '：至 ' + short(ms(a.occupied_end)));
        }
        if (a.remark) {
            lines.push('備註：' + a.remark);
        }
        lines.push('建立：' + a.created_by_name + '　最後修改：' + a.updated_by_name + ' ' + String(a.updated_at).substring(5, 16));
        return lines.join('\n');
    }

    // ---------------- PM 射程 ----------------
    function pmStatus(tool, p) {
        var s = ms(p.start), due = ms(p.due), first = ms(p.first), cat = state.b.pmCategory, done = false;
        $.each(state.actions, function (id, a) {
            if (a.tool_id === tool && a.category === cat && ms(a.start_at) < due && ms(a.occupied_end) > s) {
                done = true;
                return false;
            }
        });
        var now = serverNow();
        if (done) { return 'done'; }
        if (now > due) { return 'over'; }
        if (now > first) { return 'due'; }
        return 'wait';
    }

    function pmHtml(tool) {
        var pm = state.b.pm, list = (pm && pm.items && pm.items[tool]) || [], shown = [], h = [];
        $.each(list, function (i, p) {
            if (ms(p.start) < state.winEnd && ms(p.due) >= state.winStart) {
                shown.push(p);
            }
        });
        var n = shown.length;
        $.each(shown, function (i, p) {
            var s = ms(p.start), f = ms(p.first), due = ms(p.due), st = pmStatus(tool, p);
            var top = n > 1 ? (i * 100 / n) : 0, height = n > 1 ? (100 / n) : 100;
            var stText = { done: '已排 PM', over: '已超過最後到期日，尚未排 PM', due: '已到點，尚未排 PM', wait: '射程內' }[st];
            var title = p.type + '：射程 ' + short(s) + ' ～ ' + short(due) + '，到點 ' + short(f) + '（' + stText + '）';
            h.push('<div class="tb-pm tb-pm-' + st + '" data-pm="' + i + '" data-type="' + esc(p.type) + '" data-first="' + esc(p.first) +
                '" title="' + esc(title) + '" style="left:' + x(s) + 'px;width:' + Math.max(2, x(due) - x(s)) + 'px;top:' + top +
                '%;height:' + height + '%"><span class="tb-pm-label">' + esc(p.type) + '</span></div>');
            h.push('<div class="tb-pm-first" style="left:' + x(f) + 'px;top:' + top + '%;height:' + height + '%" title="' + esc(p.type + ' 到點 ' + short(f)) + '"><b>▼</b></div>');
            h.push('<div class="tb-pm-due" style="left:' + x(due) + 'px;top:' + top + '%;height:' + height + '%" title="' + esc(p.type + ' 最後到期 ' + short(due)) + '"><b>!</b></div>');
        });
        return h.join('');
    }

    function renderPmMsg() {
        var pm = state.b.pm, $m = $('#tb-pm-msg'), parts = [];
        if (!pm || pm.status === 'off') {
            $m.hide();
            return;
        }
        if (pm.status === 'missing' || pm.status === 'error') {
            parts.push('PM 資料：' + esc(pm.message));
        } else {
            if (pm.loose) {
                parts.push(esc(pm.message));
            }
            if (pm.skipped) {
                var more = '';
                if (pm.reasons && pm.reasons.length) {
                    more = '<details><summary>略過原因（ADMIN）</summary><ul>' +
                        $.map(pm.reasons, function (r) { return '<li>' + esc(r) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></details>';
                }
                parts.push('PM 資料：本機型 ' + pm.count + ' 筆；整個檔案略過 ' + pm.skipped + ' 筆格式錯誤的項目' + more);
            }
        }
        if (parts.length) {
            $m.html('&gt; ' + parts.join('<br>&gt; ')).show();
        } else {
            $m.hide();
        }
    }

    // ---------------- 可用率 ----------------
    function renderAvailability(av) {
        if (!av) {
            return;
        }
        state.b.availability = av;
        $wrap.find('.tb-av').each(function () {
            var i = +$(this).attr('data-day'), day = ymd(state.winStart + i * DAY);
            var v = av.values[day], hrs = av.hours ? av.hours[day] : null;
            var cls = 'tb-av' + ($(this).hasClass('tb-today') ? ' tb-today' : '');
            if (v !== null && v !== undefined) {
                if (v < av.alert) { cls += ' tb-av-alert'; } else if (v < av.warn) { cls += ' tb-av-warn'; }
            }
            $(this).attr('class', cls).text(v === null || v === undefined ? '—' : v.toFixed(1))
                .attr('title', day + (hrs !== null && hrs !== undefined ? '：可用 ' + hrs + ' h / ' + (av.tools * 24) + ' h（' + av.tools + ' 台）' : ''));
        });
        if (av.error) {
            toast(av.error, 'error');
        }
    }

    // ---------------- 圖例 / 搜尋 ----------------
    function renderLegend() {
        var b = state.b, h = ['<div class="tb-legend-row"><b>類別</b>'];
        $.each(b.categories, function (i, c) {
            h.push('<span class="tb-sw" style="background:' + esc(c.color) + '"></span>' + esc(c.label) + (c.counts_down ? '' : '（不算停機）'));
        });
        h.push('</div><div class="tb-legend-row"><b>WIP 字色</b>');
        $.each(b.wip, function (i, w) {
            h.push('<span class="tb-wip-sample" style="color:' + esc(w.text_color) + (w.bold ? ';font-weight:bold' : '') + '">' + esc(w.label) + '</span>');
        });
        h.push('</div><div class="tb-legend-row"><b>尾巴</b><span class="tb-sw tb-sw-mon"></span>Monitor<span class="tb-sw tb-sw-pil"></span>Pilot　' +
            '<b>PM</b><span class="tb-sw tb-pm-wait"></span>射程<span class="tb-sw tb-pm-done"></span>已排' +
            '<span class="tb-sw tb-pm-due"></span>已到點未排<span class="tb-sw tb-pm-over"></span>已過期未排　<b class="tb-lg-first">▼</b>到點日<b class="tb-lg-due">!</b>最後到期日</div>');
        $('#tb-legend').html(h.join(''));
    }

    function applySearch() {
        var q = $.trim($('#tb-search').val()).toUpperCase();
        $wrap.find('tbody tr[data-tool]').each(function () {
            $(this).toggle(!q || $(this).attr('data-tool').indexOf(q) >= 0);
        });
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // 右鍵選單
    // ==================================================================
    function closeMenu() { $menu.hide().empty(); }

    function openMenu(e, items) {
        var h = [];
        $.each(items, function (i, it) {
            if (it.sep) {
                h.push('<li class="tb-menu-sep"></li>');
            } else if (it.info) {
                h.push('<li class="tb-menu-info">' + esc(it.info) + '</li>');
            } else {
                h.push('<li class="tb-menu-item' + (it.danger ? ' is-danger' : '') + '" data-i="' + i + '">' + esc(it.label) + '</li>');
            }
        });
        $menu.html(h.join('')).css({ left: 0, top: 0 }).show();
        var w = $menu.outerWidth(), ht = $menu.outerHeight();
        var left = Math.min(e.pageX, $(window).scrollLeft() + $(window).width() - w - 8);
        var top = Math.min(e.pageY, $(window).scrollTop() + $(window).height() - ht - 8);
        $menu.css({ left: left, top: top });
        $menu.off('click').on('click', '.tb-menu-item', function () {
            var it = items[+$(this).attr('data-i')];
            closeMenu();
            it.run();
        });
    }

    $wrap.on('contextmenu', '.tb-lane', function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (state.drag) {
            return;
        }
        var $lane = $(this), tool = $lane.attr('data-tool');
        var dayIdx = Math.floor((e.pageX - $lane.offset().left) / state.cellW);
        var day = state.winStart + dayIdx * DAY;
        var $act = $(e.target).closest('.tb-act'), $pm = $(e.target).closest('.tb-pm');
        var items = [];
        if ($act.length) {
            var a = state.actions[+$act.attr('data-id')];
            if (!a) {
                return;
            }
            items.push({ info: a.tool_id + '  ' + catLabel(a.category) + '  ' + short(ms(a.start_at)) });
            items.push({ label: canEdit() ? '編輯' : '檢視', run: function () { openForm('edit', a); } });
            if (canEdit()) {
                items.push({ label: '複製', run: function () { openForm('copy', a); } });
                items.push({ sep: true });
                items.push({ label: '刪除', danger: true, run: function () { removeAction(a); } });
            }
        } else {
            items.push({ info: tool + '  ' + ymd(day) });
            if (canEdit()) {
                items.push({ label: '新增 Action', run: function () { openForm('new', { tool_id: tool, day: day }); } });
                if ($pm.length) {
                    var type = $pm.attr('data-type'), first = ms($pm.attr('data-first'));
                    items.push({ label: '依此 PM（' + type + '）新增 Action', run: function () {
                        openForm('new', { tool_id: tool, day: first, exact: true, category: state.b.pmCategory, pm_type: type });
                    } });
                }
            } else {
                items.push({ info: '唯讀：你沒有編輯 ' + state.b.label + ' 的權限' });
            }
        }
        openMenu(e, items);
    });
    $(document).on('mousedown', function (e) {
        if (!$(e.target).closest('#tb-menu').length) {
            closeMenu();
        }
    }).on('keydown', function (e) {
        if (e.keyCode === 27) {
            closeMenu();
        }
    });
    $wrap.on('scroll', closeMenu);

    $wrap.on('dblclick', '.tb-act', function () {
        var a = state.actions[+$(this).attr('data-id')];
        if (a) {
            openForm('edit', a);
        }
    });

    // ==================================================================
    // 表單
    // ==================================================================
    function f(name) { return $form.find('[name="' + name + '"]'); }

    function formStart() { return ms(f('start_date').val() + ' ' + (f('start_time').val() || '00:00')); }
    function formEnd() { return ms(f('end_date').val() + ' ' + (f('end_time').val() || '00:00')); }
    function setStart(t) { f('start_date').val(ymd(t)); f('start_time').val(hm(t)); }
    function setEnd(t) { f('end_date').val(ymd(t)); f('end_time').val(hm(t)); }

    function fillCategories(selected) {
        var h = [];
        $.each(state.b.categories, function (i, c) {
            h.push('<option value="' + esc(c.code) + '"' + (c.code === selected ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(c.label) + '</option>');
        });
        if (selected && !catOf(selected)) {
            h.push('<option value="" selected>（原類別 ' + esc(selected) + ' 已從設定移除，請改選）</option>');
        }
        f('category').html(h.join(''));
    }

    function fillWip(selected) {
        var h = [];
        $.each(state.b.wip, function (i, w) {
            h.push('<label class="pi-check tb-wip-opt"><input type="radio" name="wip_mode" value="' + esc(w.code) + '"' +
                (w.code === selected ? ' checked' : '') + '> <span style="color:' + esc(w.text_color) + (w.bold ? ';font-weight:bold' : '') +
                '" class="tb-wip-chip">' + esc(w.label) + '</span></label>');
        });
        if (selected && !wipOf(selected)) {
            h.push('<p class="text-red tb-wip-gone-msg">&gt; 原本的 WIP「' + esc(selected) + '」已從 ' + esc(state.b.label) + ' 的設定移除，請改選</p>');
        }
        $('#tb-f-wip').html(h.join(''));
    }

    function defaultWip(catCode) {
        var c = catOf(catCode);
        if (c && c.default_wip && wipOf(c.default_wip)) {
            return c.default_wip;
        }
        return state.b.wip.length ? state.b.wip[0].code : '';
    }

    /**
     * mode: new（data = { tool_id, day, exact?, category?, pm_type? }）/ edit（data = action）/ copy（data = action）
     */
    function openForm(mode, data) {
        closeMenu();
        var ro = !canEdit();
        var a = mode === 'new' ? null : data;
        state.modal = { mode: mode, id: mode === 'edit' ? a.id : 0, endTouched: false, wipTouched: mode !== 'new', original: null };
        $('#tb-error, #tb-stale, #tb-editors').hide();
        $('#tb-history').hide();
        $('#tb-history-list').empty();
        $form[0].reset();
        f('id').val(mode === 'edit' ? a.id : 0);
        f('version').val(mode === 'edit' ? a.version : 0);

        if (a) {
            f('tool_id').val(a.tool_id);
            fillCategories(a.category);
            fillWip(a.wip_mode);
            setStart(ms(a.start_at));
            setEnd(ms(a.end_at));
            f('need_monitor').prop('checked', a.need_monitor);
            f('monitor_hours').val(a.need_monitor ? a.monitor_hours : '');
            f('need_pilot').prop('checked', a.need_pilot);
            f('pilot_hours').val(a.need_pilot ? a.pilot_hours : '');
            f('remark').val(a.remark);
            f('pm_type').val(a.pm_type || '');
        } else {
            var cat = data.category && catOf(data.category) ? data.category : (state.b.categories[0] ? state.b.categories[0].code : '');
            var start = data.exact ? data.day : ms(ymd(data.day) + ' ' + state.b.startTime);
            if (start < state.winStart) {
                start = state.winStart;
            }
            f('tool_id').val(data.tool_id);
            fillCategories(cat);
            fillWip(defaultWip(cat));
            setStart(start);
            setEnd(start + (catOf(cat) ? catOf(cat).default_hours : 8) * HOUR);
            f('pm_type').val(data.pm_type || '');
        }

        var title = { 'new': '&gt; NEW ACTION', edit: ro ? '&gt; VIEW ACTION #' + a.id : '&gt; EDIT ACTION #' + (a ? a.id : ''), copy: '&gt; COPY ACTION' }[mode];
        $('#tb-modal-title').html(title + ' <small>' + esc(state.b.label) + '</small>');
        $form.find('input, select, textarea').not('[name=tool_id]').prop('disabled', ro);
        $('#tb-save').toggle(!ro);
        $('#tb-delete').toggle(!ro && mode === 'edit');
        $('#tb-meta').text(mode === 'edit' ? '建立：' + a.created_by_name + ' ' + String(a.created_at).substring(0, 16) +
            '　最後修改：' + a.updated_by_name + ' ' + String(a.updated_at).substring(0, 16) : '');
        syncHours();
        updateInfo();
        renderEditorsNote();
        state.modal.lastStart = formStart();
        state.modal.original = $form.serialize();
        $('body').addClass('pi-modal-open');
        $modal.show();
        if (mode === 'edit') {
            state.editing = ro ? null : { id: a.id, mode: 'edit' };
            loadHistory(a.id);
            if (!ro) {
                poll(false);
            }
        }
        if (!ro) {
            setTimeout(function () { f('category').focus(); }, 30);
        }
    }

    function closeForm(force) {
        if (!force && canEdit() && state.modal && $form.serialize() !== state.modal.original &&
            !window.confirm('有尚未儲存的修改，確定要關閉嗎？')) {
            return;
        }
        $modal.hide();
        $('body').removeClass('pi-modal-open');
        state.modal = null;
        if (state.editing && state.editing.mode === 'edit') {
            state.editing = null;
            poll(false);
        }
    }

    function syncHours() {
        var ro = !canEdit();
        $.each([['need_monitor', 'monitor_hours', '#tb-mon-box'], ['need_pilot', 'pilot_hours', '#tb-pilot-box']], function (i, p) {
            var on = f(p[0]).prop('checked');
            f(p[1]).prop('disabled', ro || !on);
            $(p[2]).toggleClass('is-off', !on).find('.tb-quick').prop('disabled', ro);
        });
    }

    /** 時長、回到生產時間 */
    function updateInfo() {
        var s = formStart(), e = formEnd();
        if (isNaN(s) || isNaN(e)) {
            $('#tb-duration, #tb-return').text('');
            return;
        }
        $('#tb-duration').text(e > s ? '時長：' + duration(e - s) : '');
        var mon = f('need_monitor').prop('checked') ? parseFloat(f('monitor_hours').val()) || 0 : 0;
        var pil = f('need_pilot').prop('checked') ? parseFloat(f('pilot_hours').val()) || 0 : 0;
        var parts = [];
        if (e > s) {
            if (mon) { parts.push('Monitor 結束（機台回到生產）：' + short(e + mon * HOUR)); }
            if (pil) { parts.push('Pilot 結束：' + short(e + (mon + pil) * HOUR)); }
            if (!mon && !pil) { parts.push('機台回到生產：' + short(e)); }
        }
        $('#tb-return').text(parts.join('　'));
    }

    function renderEditorsNote() {
        if (!state.modal || !state.modal.id) {
            $('#tb-editors').hide();
            return;
        }
        var eds = state.editors[state.modal.id];
        if (eds && eds.length) {
            $('#tb-editors').text('> ' + $.map(eds, function (x2) { return x2.name; }).join('、') + ' 也正在編輯這筆，存檔時若對方已先存，會提示衝突').show();
        } else {
            $('#tb-editors').hide();
        }
    }

    function loadHistory(id) {
        $.getJSON(CFG.urls.history, { id: id }).done(function (res) {
            if (!state.modal || state.modal.id !== id || !res.logs.length) {
                return;
            }
            var ops = { create: '新增', update: '修改', move: '拖曳', 'delete': '刪除' };
            $('#tb-history-list').html($.map(res.logs, function (l) {
                var dt = l.detail || {}, a2 = dt.after, b2 = dt.before, desc = '';
                if (a2 && (l.op === 'move' || (b2 && (a2.start_at !== b2.start_at || a2.end_at !== b2.end_at)))) {
                    desc = (b2 ? short(ms(b2.start_at)) + '～' + short(ms(b2.end_at)) + ' → ' : '') + short(ms(a2.start_at)) + '～' + short(ms(a2.end_at));
                } else if (a2) {
                    desc = short(ms(a2.start_at)) + '～' + short(ms(a2.end_at));
                }
                return '<li>' + esc(String(l.at).substring(5, 16)) + '　' + esc(l.by) + '　' + esc(ops[l.op] || l.op) +
                    (dt.overwrite ? '（覆蓋）' : '') + (desc ? '　<span class="dim">' + esc(desc) + '</span>' : '') + '</li>';
            }).join(''));
            $('#tb-history').show();
        });
    }

    $form.on('change input', '[name=start_date], [name=start_time]', function () {
        // 改開始時間：結束時間跟著平移、時長不變（手動改過結束時間後就不再平移）
        var m = state.modal;
        if (!m) {
            return;
        }
        var s = formStart();
        if (!m.endTouched && !isNaN(s) && m.lastStart !== undefined && !isNaN(m.lastStart)) {
            var e = formEnd();
            if (!isNaN(e)) {
                setEnd(e + (s - m.lastStart));
            }
        }
        m.lastStart = s;
        updateInfo();
    }).on('focus', '[name=start_date], [name=start_time]', function () {
        if (state.modal) {
            state.modal.lastStart = formStart();
        }
    }).on('change input', '[name=end_date], [name=end_time]', function () {
        if (state.modal) {
            state.modal.endTouched = true;
        }
        updateInfo();
    }).on('change', '[name=category]', function () {
        var m = state.modal, c = catOf(this.value);
        if (!m || !c) {
            return;
        }
        if (m.mode === 'new' && !m.endTouched) {
            setEnd(formStart() + c.default_hours * HOUR);
        }
        if (!m.wipTouched) {
            fillWip(defaultWip(c.code));
        }
        updateInfo();
    }).on('change', '[name=wip_mode]', function () {
        if (state.modal) {
            state.modal.wipTouched = true;
        }
    }).on('change', '[name=need_monitor], [name=need_pilot]', function () {
        syncHours();
        var box = this.name === 'need_monitor' ? 'monitor_hours' : 'pilot_hours';
        if (this.checked && !f(box).val()) {
            f(box).focus();
        }
        updateInfo();
    }).on('input change', '[name=monitor_hours], [name=pilot_hours]', updateInfo);

    $form.on('click', '.tb-quick', function () {
        var $t = $('#' + $(this).attr('data-target'));
        $t.val($(this).attr('data-h'));
        f($t.attr('name') === 'monitor_hours' ? 'need_monitor' : 'need_pilot').prop('checked', true);
        syncHours();
        updateInfo();
    });

    function formPayload() {
        var s = formStart(), e = formEnd();
        return {
            id: f('id').val(),
            version: f('version').val(),
            model: state.model,
            tool_id: f('tool_id').val(),
            category: f('category').val(),
            wip_mode: $form.find('[name=wip_mode]:checked').val() || '',
            start_at: isNaN(s) ? '' : full(s),
            end_at: isNaN(e) ? '' : full(e),
            need_monitor: f('need_monitor').prop('checked') ? '1' : '0',
            monitor_hours: f('monitor_hours').val(),
            need_pilot: f('need_pilot').prop('checked') ? '1' : '0',
            pilot_hours: f('pilot_hours').val(),
            remark: f('remark').val(),
            pm_type: f('pm_type').val()
        };
    }

    function showFormError(msg) {
        $('#tb-error').text('> ' + msg).show();
        $modal.scrollTop(0);
    }

    function save(payload) {
        if (!payload.wip_mode) {
            return showFormError('請勾選 WIP 處理方式');
        }
        if (!payload.start_at || !payload.end_at) {
            return showFormError('請填寫開始與結束時間');
        }
        if (ms(payload.end_at) <= ms(payload.start_at)) {
            return showFormError('結束時間必須晚於開始時間');
        }
        var $btn = $('#tb-save').prop('disabled', true);
        $.post(CFG.urls.save, payload).done(function (res) {
            upsert(res.action);
            closeForm(true);
            toast(payload.id > 0 ? '已儲存' : '已新增 ' + res.action.tool_id + ' ' + catLabel(res.action.category), 'ok');
            poll(false, true);
        }).fail(function (xhr) {
            var r = xhr.responseJSON || {};
            if (xhr.status === 409) {
                conflict(payload, r, 'form');
            } else if (xhr.status !== 401) {
                showFormError(errorText(xhr, '儲存失敗'));
            }
        }).always(function () {
            $btn.prop('disabled', false);
        });
    }

    $form.on('submit', function () { save(formPayload()); });
    $('#tb-cancel, #tb-cancel-x').on('click', function () { closeForm(false); });
    $modal.on('mousedown', function (e) {
        if (e.target === this) {
            closeForm(false);
        }
    });
    $(document).on('keydown', function (e) {
        if (e.keyCode === 27 && $modal.is(':visible') && !$conflict.is(':visible')) {
            closeForm(false);
        }
    });
    $('#tb-delete').on('click', function () {
        var a = state.actions[+f('id').val()];
        if (a) {
            removeAction(a, true);
        }
    });
    $('#tb-stale-load').on('click', function () {
        var a = state.actions[state.modal ? state.modal.id : 0];
        if (a) {
            openForm('edit', a);
        }
    });

    function removeAction(a, fromForm) {
        if (!window.confirm('確定刪除 ' + a.tool_id + ' 的「' + catLabel(a.category) + '」（' + short(ms(a.start_at)) + ' ～ ' + short(ms(a.end_at)) + '）？')) {
            return;
        }
        $.post(CFG.urls['delete'], { id: a.id, version: a.version }).done(function () {
            drop(a.id);
            if (fromForm) {
                closeForm(true);
            }
            toast('已刪除', 'ok');
            poll(false, true);
        }).fail(function (xhr) {
            var r = xhr.responseJSON || {};
            if (xhr.status === 409 && r.action && !r.action.deleted) {
                upsert(r.action);
                toast(r.error + '，請確認後再刪除', 'error');
                if (fromForm) {
                    openForm('edit', r.action);
                }
            } else if (xhr.status === 409) {
                drop(a.id);
                if (fromForm) {
                    closeForm(true);
                }
                toast(errorText(xhr), 'error');
            } else if (xhr.status !== 401) {
                toast(errorText(xhr, '刪除失敗'), 'error');
            }
        });
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // 衝突（409）
    // ==================================================================
    /**
     * mine: 我要送出的內容（save 的 payload 或 move 的 {start_at, end_at}）
     * kind: form / drag
     */
    function conflict(mine, r, kind) {
        var latest = r.action;
        if (!latest || latest.deleted || r.reason === 'gone') {
            if (latest) {
                drop(latest.id);
            } else if (mine.id) {
                drop(+mine.id);
            }
            if (kind === 'form') {
                closeForm(true);
            }
            toast(r.error || '這筆已被刪除', 'error');
            return;
        }
        upsertSilently(latest);
        var full2 = kind === 'drag' ? $.extend({}, latest, { start_at: mine.start_at, end_at: mine.end_at }) : mine;
        var rows = [
            ['類別', catLabel(full2.category), catLabel(latest.category)],
            ['WIP', wipLabel(full2.wip_mode), wipLabel(latest.wip_mode)],
            ['開始', String(full2.start_at).substring(0, 16), String(latest.start_at).substring(0, 16)],
            ['結束', String(full2.end_at).substring(0, 16), String(latest.end_at).substring(0, 16)],
            ['Monitor', tailText(full2.need_monitor, full2.monitor_hours), tailText(latest.need_monitor, latest.monitor_hours)],
            ['Pilot', tailText(full2.need_pilot, full2.pilot_hours), tailText(latest.need_pilot, latest.pilot_hours)],
            ['備註', full2.remark || '', latest.remark || '']
        ];
        $('#tb-conflict-msg').text('> ' + r.error + '。要用你的版本覆蓋，還是使用最新版本？');
        $('#tb-conflict-rows').html($.map(rows, function (row) {
            return '<tr class="' + (row[1] !== row[2] ? 'pi-diff-hot' : '') + '"><td>' + esc(row[0]) + '</td><td>' + esc(row[1]) + '</td><td>' + esc(row[2]) + '</td></tr>';
        }).join(''));
        $conflict.show();
        $('#tb-conflict-overwrite').off('click').on('click', function () {
            $conflict.hide();
            if (kind === 'form') {
                f('version').val(latest.version);
                save($.extend({}, mine, { version: latest.version, overwrite: '1' }));
            } else {
                sendMove(latest, mine.start_at, mine.end_at, true);
            }
        });
        $('#tb-conflict-load').off('click').on('click', function () {
            $conflict.hide();
            upsert(latest);
            if (kind === 'form') {
                openForm('edit', latest);
            }
        });
        $('#tb-conflict-cancel').off('click').on('click', function () {
            $conflict.hide();
            if (kind === 'drag') {
                upsert(latest);
            } else {
                f('version').val(latest.version);   // 繼續編輯，下次存檔以最新版本為基準
            }
        });
    }
    function tailText(on, h) { return on ? hours(h) : '—'; }

    // ==================================================================
    // 資料更新
    // ==================================================================
    function upsertSilently(a) {
        if (inWindow(a)) {
            state.actions[a.id] = a;
        } else {
            delete state.actions[a.id];
        }
    }
    function upsert(a) {
        var old = state.actions[a.id];
        upsertSilently(a);
        renderLane(a.tool_id);
        if (old && old.tool_id !== a.tool_id) {
            renderLane(old.tool_id);
        }
    }
    function drop(id) {
        var old = state.actions[id];
        delete state.actions[id];
        if (old) {
            renderLane(old.tool_id);
        }
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // 拖曳：中間 = 搬移；左右邊 = 改開始 / 結束；預設以天對齊，按住 Shift 以小時對齊
    // ==================================================================
    $wrap.on('mousedown', '.tb-act.is-editable', function (e) {
        if (e.which !== 1 || !canEdit()) {
            return;
        }
        var $act = $(this), a = state.actions[+$act.attr('data-id')];
        if (!a) {
            return;
        }
        e.preventDefault();
        closeMenu();
        var $lane = state.lanes[a.tool_id];
        // 色塊夠寬才能拉邊（左右各 7px），太窄的色塊一律整段搬移
        var $main = $act.children('.tb-act-main'), mw = $main.outerWidth(), ox = e.pageX - $main.offset().left, mode = 'm';
        if (mw >= 24 && ox <= 7) {
            mode = 'l';
        } else if (mw >= 24 && ox >= mw - 7 && ox <= mw) {
            mode = 'r';
        }
        state.drag = {
            a: a,
            mode: mode,
            $act: $act,
            $lane: $lane,
            x0: e.pageX - $lane.offset().left,
            s0: ms(a.start_at),
            e0: ms(a.end_at),
            tail: tailMs(a),
            started: false,
            s: ms(a.start_at),
            e: ms(a.end_at),
            bad: false
        };
    });

    $(document).on('mousemove', function (e) {
        var g = state.drag;
        if (!g) {
            return;
        }
        var lx = e.pageX - g.$lane.offset().left, dx = lx - g.x0;
        if (!g.started) {
            if (Math.abs(dx) < 4) {
                return;
            }
            g.started = true;
            g.$act.addClass('tb-dragging');
            $('body').addClass('tb-is-dragging');
            state.editing = { id: g.a.id, mode: 'drag' };
            poll(false);
        }
        var unit = e.shiftKey ? HOUR : DAY;
        var delta = Math.round(dx / state.cellW * DAY / unit) * unit;
        var s = g.s0, en = g.e0;
        if (g.mode === 'm') {
            s = g.s0 + delta;
            en = g.e0 + delta;
            if (s < state.winStart && s !== g.s0) {
                en += state.winStart - s;
                s = state.winStart;
            }
        } else if (g.mode === 'l') {
            s = Math.min(g.s0 + delta, g.e0 - HOUR);
            if (s < state.winStart && s !== g.s0) {
                s = Math.min(state.winStart, g.e0 - HOUR);
            }
        } else {
            en = Math.max(g.e0 + delta, g.s0 + HOUR);
        }
        if (s >= state.winEnd) {
            var shift = s - (state.winEnd - unit);
            s -= shift;
            if (g.mode === 'm') {
                en -= shift;
            }
        }
        g.s = s;
        g.e = en;
        g.bad = overlaps(g.a, s, en + g.tail);
        // 更新畫面
        var cw = state.cellW, mainW = Math.max(3, (en - s) / DAY * cw);
        var monW = g.a.need_monitor ? g.a.monitor_hours * HOUR / DAY * cw : 0;
        var pilW = g.a.need_pilot ? g.a.pilot_hours * HOUR / DAY * cw : 0;
        g.$act.css({ left: x(s), width: mainW + monW + pilW }).toggleClass('tb-bad', g.bad);
        g.$act.children('.tb-act-main').css('width', mainW);
        g.$act.children('.tb-tail-mon').css('left', mainW);
        g.$act.children('.tb-tail-pil').css('left', mainW + monW);
        $tip.text(short(s) + ' → ' + short(en) + (g.tail ? '（尾巴至 ' + short(en + g.tail) + '）' : '') +
            (g.bad ? '　✕ 與其他 Action 重疊' : '') + (e.shiftKey ? '　[小時]' : '　[天]，按住 Shift 以小時對齊'))
            .toggleClass('is-bad', g.bad).css({ left: e.pageX + 14, top: e.pageY + 16 }).show();
        // 拖到邊緣自動捲動
        var wl = $wrap.offset().left, wr = wl + $wrap.innerWidth();
        if (e.pageX > wr - 40) {
            $wrap.scrollLeft($wrap.scrollLeft() + 16);
        } else if (e.pageX < wl + TOOL_COL + 40) {
            $wrap.scrollLeft($wrap.scrollLeft() - 16);
        }
    }).on('mouseup', function () {
        var g = state.drag;
        if (!g) {
            return;
        }
        state.drag = null;
        $tip.hide();
        $('body').removeClass('tb-is-dragging');
        if (!g.started) {
            return;
        }
        state.editing = null;
        if (g.bad || (g.s === g.s0 && g.e === g.e0)) {
            if (g.bad) {
                toast('與同台其他 Action（含 Monitor / Pilot 尾巴）重疊，已還原', 'error');
            }
            renderLane(g.a.tool_id);
            flushQueue();
            poll(false);
            return;
        }
        sendMove(g.a, full(g.s), full(g.e), false);
        flushQueue();
    });

    function overlaps(a, s, occ) {
        var hit = false;
        $.each(state.actions, function (id, o) {
            if (o.id !== a.id && o.tool_id === a.tool_id && ms(o.start_at) < occ && ms(o.occupied_end) > s) {
                hit = true;
                return false;
            }
        });
        return hit;
    }

    function sendMove(a, startAt, endAt, overwrite) {
        var payload = { id: a.id, version: a.version, start_at: startAt, end_at: endAt };
        if (overwrite) {
            payload.overwrite = '1';
        }
        $.post(CFG.urls.move, payload).done(function (res) {
            upsert(res.action);
            toast(res.action.tool_id + ' 已改為 ' + short(ms(res.action.start_at)) + ' ～ ' + short(ms(res.action.end_at)), 'ok');
            poll(false, true);
        }).fail(function (xhr) {
            var r = xhr.responseJSON || {};
            renderLane(a.tool_id);
            if (xhr.status === 409) {
                conflict({ id: a.id, start_at: startAt, end_at: endAt }, r, 'drag');
            } else if (xhr.status !== 401) {
                if (r.action) {
                    upsert(r.action);
                }
                toast(errorText(xhr, '拖曳失敗，已還原'), 'error');
            }
        });
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // 輪詢
    // ==================================================================
    function poll(force, withAvail) {
        if (!state.b || (state.pollXhr && !force)) {
            return;
        }
        if (state.pollXhr) {
            state.pollXhr.abort();
        }
        var model = state.model;
        state.pollXhr = $.getJSON(CFG.urls.poll, {
            model: model,
            since: state.cursor,
            editing: state.editing ? state.editing.id : 0,
            mode: state.editing ? state.editing.mode : 'view',
            pm_sig: state.b.pm ? state.b.pm.sig : '',
            avail: withAvail ? 1 : 0
        }).done(function (res) {
            if (model !== state.model) {
                return;
            }
            if (state.drag && state.drag.started) {
                state.queued.push(res);
                return;
            }
            applyPoll(res);
            if (!state.paused) {
                setLive('live');
            }
        }).fail(function (xhr, st) {
            if (st !== 'abort' && xhr.status !== 401) {
                setLive('error', '同步失敗，重試中');
            }
        }).always(function () {
            state.pollXhr = null;
        });
    }

    function flushQueue() {
        var q = state.queued;
        state.queued = [];
        $.each(q, function (i, res) { applyPoll(res); });
    }

    function applyPoll(res) {
        if (res.today !== state.b.today) {
            // 跨日：時間視窗移動，整個重新載入
            load(state.model);
            return;
        }
        state.cursor = res.cursor;
        var dirty = {};
        $.each(res.changed, function (i, a) {
            var old = state.actions[a.id];
            if (old && old.version >= a.version) {
                return;
            }
            if (old) {
                dirty[old.tool_id] = true;
            }
            upsertSilently(a);
            dirty[a.tool_id] = true;
            noteStale(a, false);
        });
        $.each(res.deleted, function (i, id) {
            var old = state.actions[id];
            if (old) {
                delete state.actions[id];
                dirty[old.tool_id] = true;
                noteStale(old, true);
            }
        });
        // 編輯中提示
        var oldEds = state.editors;
        state.editors = res.editors || {};
        $.each([oldEds, state.editors], function (k, map) {
            $.each(map, function (id) {
                var a = state.actions[id];
                if (a) {
                    dirty[a.tool_id] = true;
                }
            });
        });
        if (res.canEdit !== state.b.canEdit) {
            state.b.canEdit = res.canEdit;
            renderMode();
            dirty = {};
            $.each(state.b.tools, function (i, t) { dirty[t] = true; });
        }
        if (res.pm) {
            state.b.pm = res.pm;
            renderPmMsg();
            $.each(state.b.tools, function (i, t) { dirty[t] = true; });
        }
        if (res.changed.length || res.deleted.length) {
            // PM 狀態（已排 / 未排）可能改變
            $.each(state.b.tools, function (i, t) {
                if (state.b.pm && state.b.pm.items && state.b.pm.items[t]) {
                    dirty[t] = true;
                }
            });
        }
        $.each(dirty, function (tool) { renderLane(tool); });
        if (res.availability) {
            renderAvailability(res.availability);
        }
        $('#tb-online').html(res.online.length ? '線上：<b>' + esc(res.online.join('、')) + '</b>' : '');
        renderEditorsNote();
    }

    /** 別人改了我正在編輯的那一筆：立刻提示 */
    function noteStale(a, gone) {
        if (!state.modal || state.modal.id !== a.id) {
            return;
        }
        if (!gone && a.version <= +f('version').val()) {
            return;
        }
        $('#tb-stale-text').text(gone ? '> 這筆已被刪除，存檔將會失敗' : '> 這筆已被 ' + a.updated_by_name + ' 於 ' + String(a.updated_at).substring(5, 16) + ' 更新');
        $('#tb-stale-load').toggle(!gone);
        $('#tb-stale').show();
    }

    // 閒置偵測：超過 idleSeconds 沒操作，或分頁在背景，就暫停輪詢，讓 session 照常逾時
    function isHidden() { return document.hidden === true || document.msHidden === true; }
    function pause(reason) {
        state.paused = true;
        setLive('paused', reason);
    }
    function resume() {
        state.paused = false;
        setLive('live');
        poll(true, true);
    }
    function tick() {
        if (isHidden()) {
            pause('已暫停同步（分頁在背景）');
        } else if (nowMs() - state.lastActivity > CFG.idleSeconds * 1000) {
            pause('已暫停同步（閒置）');
        } else {
            poll(false);
        }
    }
    $(document).on('mousemove keydown mousedown wheel touchstart', function () {
        state.lastActivity = nowMs();
        if (state.paused && !isHidden()) {
            resume();
        }
    });
    $(document).on('visibilitychange msvisibilitychange', function () {
        if (isHidden()) {
            pause('已暫停同步（分頁在背景）');
        } else if (nowMs() - state.lastActivity <= CFG.idleSeconds * 1000) {
            resume();
        }
    });
    // 每分鐘重畫「現在」的紅線位置與 PM 狀態
    setInterval(function () {
        if (state.b && !state.drag) {
            state.laneBg = laneBackground(Math.round((state.todayMs - state.winStart) / DAY));
            $.each(state.b.tools, function (i, t) { renderLane(t); });
        }
    }, 60000);

    // 離開頁面時清除自己的線上 / 編輯中
    $(window).on('pagehide beforeunload', function () {
        if (navigator.sendBeacon && window.FormData) {
            var fd = new FormData();
            fd.append('_csrf', $('meta[name="csrf-token"]').attr('content') || '');
            navigator.sendBeacon(CFG.urls.release, fd);
        }
    });

    // ==================================================================
    // 其他操作
    // ==================================================================
    $('#tb-tabs').on('click', '.pi-tab', function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        if ($modal.is(':visible')) {
            return;
        }
        var m = String($(this).attr('data-model'));
        if (m !== state.model) {
            load(m);
        }
    });
    $('#tb-search').on('input', applySearch);
    $('#tb-legend-btn').on('click', function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        $('#tb-legend').toggle();
    });
    $('#tb-ie-ok').on('click', function () { $('#tb-ie').hide(); });

    // ==================================================================
    // 啟動
    // ==================================================================
    if (!CFG.models.length) {
        $wrap.html('<div class="text-red tb-loading">&gt; config/tool_board.ini 沒有設定機型</div>');
        return;
    }
    if (IS_IE) {
        $('#tb-ie').show();
    }
    load(pickModel());
    setInterval(tick, CFG.pollSeconds * 1000);
})(window.jQuery);
#@@@ FILE scripts/python/check_config.py sha1=c406a41ba8da40a1e4a68911ca289f7cb2abe13c base=cbb267081080c75375821ab7b054586370a551d7 mode=755 enc=text nl=1
#!/usr/bin/env python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
檢查 config/config.ini 是否正確（Python 2.7）

檢查項目：
  - [access] 的每個身份都定義在 [roles]，列出的頁面都有 [page.xxx]
  - 每個 [page.xxx] 的 route 指向的檔案存在
      module:<資料夾>  → modules/<資料夾>/controller.php
      pages/xxx.php    → 檔案存在
      public/xxx.php   → 檔案存在，且第一段程式有 require auth_check.php
  - action.<動作> 的身份都有效
  - 頁面的 group 有對應的 [group.xxx]，[group.xxx] 有被頁面使用（警告）
  - [register] 的 listed_role / unlisted_role 有效且不是超級身份 ("*")
  - [ee_accounts] 名單帳號格式正確、沒有重複
  - 沒有任何身份能使用的頁面、modules/ 下沒登記的資料夾（警告）
  - 圖表（route = module:charts 的頁面）：charts/<名稱>.ini 存在且格式正確、
    source = php 時 charts/<名稱>.php 存在且有 <名稱>Chart 類別、
    charts/ 底下沒有頁面使用的 chart（警告）
  - 借機 / PM 看板（有 [page.tool_board] 時）：config/tool_board.ini 與每個機型的
    config/tool_board/<機型>.ini 存在且格式正確、顏色是 Hex、機台沒有重複列在多個機型、
    每個機型至少一個 [wip.*]、[editors] 帳號是否在 [ee_accounts]（警告）、
    可用率公式檔存在、PM JSON 檔可解析（單引號格式只警告）
  - 列出每個身份可用的頁面

用法：python scripts/python/check_config.py
回傳碼：0 = 正常，1 = 有錯誤
"""
from __future__ import print_function

import os
import re
import sys
from ConfigParser import RawConfigParser  # Python 2.7；RawConfigParser 不會把 % 當成變數

ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..'))
INI = os.path.join(ROOT, 'config', 'config.ini')
MODULES = os.path.join(ROOT, 'modules')
CHARTS = os.path.join(ROOT, 'charts')
RE_CHART = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,31}$')
RE_PLOT = re.compile(r'^plot\.[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,63}$')
RE_KEY = re.compile(r'^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,63}$')
RE_ACCOUNT = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{3,64}$')

GREEN = '\033[32m'
RED = '\033[31m'
YELLOW = '\033[33m'
RESET = '\033[0m'


def unquote(value):
    value = value.strip()
    if not value.startswith('"'):
        value = value.split(';', 1)[0].strip()
    if len(value) >= 2 and value[0] == '"' and value[-1] == '"':
        value = value[1:-1]
    return value


def split_list(value):
    return [v.strip() for v in unquote(value).split(',') if v.strip()]


def read_array(section_name, key, path=None):
    """account[] 是 PHP 陣列語法，ConfigParser 遇到重複 key 只留最後一個，所以逐行讀"""
    values = []
    section = None
    pattern = re.compile(r'^' + re.escape(key) + r'\[\]\s*=')
    with open(path or INI) as fp:
        for line in fp:
            line = line.strip()
            if line.startswith('['):
                section = line.strip('[]')
            elif section == section_name and pattern.match(line):
                values.append(unquote(line.split('=', 1)[1]))
    return values


def chart_names():
    """charts/ 底下的 chart 名稱（不含 _ 開頭的範本）"""
    if not os.path.isdir(CHARTS):
        return []
    return sorted(f[:-4] for f in os.listdir(CHARTS) if f.endswith('.ini') and RE_CHART.match(f[:-4]))


DB_KEYS = ('ip', 'port', 'name', 'user', 'password', 'charset')
SPEC_KEYS = ('upper_oos', 'upper_ooc', 'lower_ooc', 'lower_oos')


def check_chart_db(label, parser, get, errors):
    """[db]：只能有 DB_KEYS；port 為整數；ip 留空時 name / user / password 也要留空"""
    for opt in parser.options('db'):
        if opt not in DB_KEYS:
            errors.append('%s [db] 不認得的欄位：%s（可用：%s）' % (label, opt, '、'.join(DB_KEYS)))
    ip = get('db', 'ip')
    if ip and not re.match(r'^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]+$', ip):
        errors.append('%s [db] ip 格式錯誤（只能是 IP 或主機名稱）' % label)
    port = get('db', 'port')
    if port and (not port.isdigit() or not 1 <= int(port) <= 65535):
        errors.append('%s [db] port 必須是 1 ~ 65535 的整數' % label)
    for key in ('name', 'charset'):
        v = get('db', key)
        if v and not re.match(r'^[A-Za-z0-9_$-]+$', v):
            errors.append('%s [db] %s 只能有英文、數字、底線、$、-' % (label, key))
    if not ip:
        for key in ('name', 'user', 'password'):
            if get('db', key):
                errors.append('%s [db] 有填 %s 但沒有填 ip（ip 留空 = 使用 AISLE 自己的資料庫）' % (label, key))


def check_spec(label, section, get, errors):
    """spec 線：數字或留空；lower_oos <= lower_ooc < upper_ooc <= upper_oos"""
    vals = {}
    for key in SPEC_KEYS:
        v = get(section, key)
        if not v:
            continue
        try:
            vals[key] = float(v)
        except ValueError:
            errors.append('%s [%s] %s 必須是數字或留空：%s' % (label, section, key, v))
    for i, a in enumerate(SPEC_KEYS):
        for b in SPEC_KEYS[i + 1:]:
            if a not in vals or b not in vals:
                continue
            same_side = a[:5] == b[:5]
            if (vals[a] < vals[b]) if same_side else (vals[a] <= vals[b]):
                errors.append('%s [%s] %s（%s）必須%s %s（%s）' % (
                    label, section, a, vals[a], '大於等於' if same_side else '大於', b, vals[b]))


def check_chart(name, errors, warnings):
    """檢查一張 chart 的 ini 與 php"""
    label = 'charts/%s.ini' % name
    parser = RawConfigParser()
    parser.optionxform = str
    try:
        with open(os.path.join(CHARTS, name + '.ini')) as fp:
            parser.readfp(fp)
    except Exception as e:  # 語法錯誤
        errors.append('%s 解析失敗：%s' % (label, e))
        return

    def get(section, key):
        return unquote(parser.get(section, key)) if parser.has_option(section, key) else ''

    source = (get('page', 'source') or 'php').lower() if parser.has_section('page') else 'php'
    if source not in ('php', 'sample'):
        errors.append('%s [page] source 只能是 php 或 sample' % label)
    for key in ('default_days', 'max_days'):
        v = get('page', key) if parser.has_section('page') else ''
        if v and (not v.isdigit() or int(v) < 1):
            errors.append('%s [page] %s 必須是正整數' % (label, key))

    if parser.has_section('db'):
        check_chart_db(label, parser, get, errors)

    plots = 0
    for section in parser.sections():
        if section in ('page', 'db'):
            continue
        if not RE_PLOT.match(section):
            errors.append('%s 區段名稱錯誤：[%s]，應為 [page]、[db] 或 [plot.<小寫代號>]' % (label, section))
            continue
        plots += 1
        if not split_list(get(section, 'params')):
            errors.append('%s [%s] 沒有設定 params' % (label, section))
        for opt in parser.options(section):
            if opt.endswith('ymin') or opt.endswith('ymax'):
                v = get(section, opt)
                if v:
                    try:
                        float(v)
                    except ValueError:
                        errors.append('%s [%s] %s 必須是數字：%s' % (label, section, opt, v))
        check_spec(label, section, get, errors)
    if plots == 0:
        errors.append('%s 沒有任何 [plot.xxx] 圖' % label)

    php = os.path.join(CHARTS, name + '.php')
    if source == 'php':
        if not os.path.isfile(php):
            errors.append('charts/%s.php 不存在（尚未寫好時可先把 ini 的 source 設為 sample）' % name)
        else:
            with open(php) as fp:
                code = fp.read()
            if not re.search(r'class\s+' + re.escape(name) + r'Chart\s+extends\s+ChartSource\b', code):
                errors.append('charts/%s.php 裡找不到 class %sChart extends ChartSource' % (name, name))
            if '尚未實作' in code:
                warnings.append('charts/%s.php 似乎還沒寫好 SQL（仍有「尚未實作」），但 ini 的 source = php' % name)


RE_HEX = re.compile(r'^#([0-9A-Fa-f]{3}|[0-9A-Fa-f]{6})$')
RE_TOOL = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.\-]{0,63}$')
RE_CODE = re.compile(r'^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,31}$')


def check_tool_board(ee_accounts, errors, warnings):
    """借機 / PM 看板設定"""
    shared_file = os.path.join(ROOT, 'config', 'tool_board.ini')
    label = 'config/tool_board.ini'
    if not os.path.isfile(shared_file):
        errors.append('找不到 %s' % label)
        return
    shared = RawConfigParser()
    try:
        with open(shared_file) as fp:
            shared.readfp(fp)
    except Exception as e:  # noqa
        errors.append('%s 解析失敗：%s' % (label, e))
        return

    def sget(parser, section, key):
        return unquote(parser.get(section, key)) if parser.has_option(section, key) else ''

    models = [m.upper() for m in shared.options('models')] if shared.has_section('models') else []
    if not models:
        errors.append('%s 的 [models] 沒有任何機型' % label)

    def check_categories(parser, where):
        codes = []
        for section in parser.sections():
            if not section.startswith('category.'):
                continue
            code = section[len('category.'):]
            codes.append(code)
            if not RE_CODE.match(code):
                errors.append('%s [%s] 類別代碼只能用小寫英數字與底線' % (where, section))
            color = sget(parser, section, 'color')
            if color and not RE_HEX.match(color):
                errors.append('%s [%s] color "%s" 不是 Hex 色號（例 "#E67E22"）' % (where, section, color))
        return codes

    categories = check_categories(shared, label)
    if not categories:
        errors.append('%s 沒有任何 [category.<代碼>]' % label)
    pm_category = sget(shared, 'pm', 'category') or 'pm'
    if pm_category not in categories:
        warnings.append('%s [pm] category "%s" 不是已定義的類別' % (label, pm_category))

    formula = (sget(shared, 'availability', 'formula') or 'default').lower()
    if formula != 'default':
        camel = ''.join(w.capitalize() for w in formula.split('_'))
        if not os.path.isfile(os.path.join(MODULES, 'tool_board', 'formulas', camel + '.php')):
            errors.append('%s [availability] formula "%s" 找不到 modules/tool_board/formulas/%s.php' % (label, formula, camel))

    pm_path = sget(shared, 'pm', 'json_path')
    if pm_path:
        if not os.path.isfile(pm_path):
            warnings.append('%s [pm] json_path 檔案不存在：%s（看板會顯示警告，其他功能正常）' % (label, pm_path))
        else:
            import json
            import ast
            with open(pm_path) as fp:
                text = fp.read()
            try:
                json.loads(text)
            except ValueError:
                try:
                    ast.literal_eval(text)
                    warnings.append('PM 檔 %s 不是標準 JSON（單引號），看板會自動轉換；請產生端改用 json.dump' % pm_path)
                except Exception:  # noqa
                    errors.append('PM 檔 %s 無法解析' % pm_path)

    owner = {}
    defaults_wip = [(c, sget(shared, 'category.' + c, 'default_wip')) for c in categories]
    for m in models:
        rel = 'config/tool_board/%s.ini' % m
        path = os.path.join(ROOT, rel)
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            errors.append('找不到機型設定檔 %s' % rel)
            continue
        parser = RawConfigParser()
        try:
            with open(path) as fp:
                parser.readfp(fp)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa
            errors.append('%s 解析失敗：%s' % (rel, e))
            continue
        tools = read_array('tools', 'tool', path)
        if not tools:
            warnings.append('%s 的 [tools] 沒有任何機台' % rel)
        for t in tools:
            if not RE_TOOL.match(t):
                errors.append('%s 機台代號格式錯誤：%s' % (rel, t))
                continue
            key = t.upper()
            if key in owner and owner[key] != m:
                errors.append('機台 %s 同時列在 %s 與 %s' % (key, owner[key], m))
            elif key in owner:
                warnings.append('%s 機台重複：%s' % (rel, key))
            owner[key] = m
        editors = read_array('editors', 'account', path)
        if not editors:
            warnings.append('%s 的 [editors] 沒有任何帳號，這個機型只有 ADMIN 能編輯' % rel)
        for a in editors:
            if not RE_ACCOUNT.match(a):
                errors.append('%s [editors] 帳號格式錯誤：%s' % (rel, a))
            elif a.lower() not in ee_accounts:
                warnings.append('%s [editors] %s 不在 [ee_accounts]，若不是 EE 帳號將無法編輯' % (rel, a))
        wips = []
        for section in parser.sections():
            if section.startswith('wip.'):
                code = section[len('wip.'):]
                wips.append(code)
                if not RE_CODE.match(code):
                    errors.append('%s [%s] WIP 代碼只能用小寫英數字與底線' % (rel, section))
                color = sget(parser, section, 'text_color')
                if color and not RE_HEX.match(color):
                    errors.append('%s [%s] text_color "%s" 不是 Hex 色號' % (rel, section, color))
        if not wips:
            errors.append('%s 沒有任何 [wip.<代碼>]（WIP 處理方式）' % rel)
        check_categories(parser, rel)
        for cat, dw in defaults_wip:
            if dw and dw not in wips:
                warnings.append('%s 沒有 [wip.%s]，類別 %s 的 default_wip 在這個機型改選第一個' % (rel, dw, cat))
        print('== 借機看板 %s：機台 %d 台、可編輯 %s、WIP %s ==' % (
            m, len(tools), ', '.join(editors) or '-', ', '.join(wips) or '-'))


def main():
    errors = []
    warnings = []

    parser = RawConfigParser()
    parser.optionxform = str  # 保留 key 大小寫（EE / PE）
    with open(INI) as fp:
        parser.readfp(fp)

    def get(section, key):
        return unquote(parser.get(section, key)) if parser.has_option(section, key) else ''

    roles = parser.options('roles') if parser.has_section('roles') else []

    # 選單分類
    groups = set()
    for section in parser.sections():
        if section.startswith('group.'):
            key = section[len('group.'):]
            if not RE_KEY.match(key):
                errors.append('[%s] 分類代號格式錯誤' % section)
            groups.add(key)
    used_groups = set()

    # 頁面
    pages = {}
    for section in parser.sections():
        if not section.startswith('page.'):
            continue
        key = section[len('page.'):]
        if not RE_KEY.match(key):
            errors.append('[%s] 頁面代號格式錯誤' % section)
            continue
        route = get(section, 'route') or ('module:' + key)
        pages[key] = route
        if route.startswith('module:'):
            target = os.path.join(MODULES, route[len('module:'):].strip(), 'controller.php')
            if not os.path.isfile(target):
                errors.append('[%s] 找不到 %s' % (section, os.path.relpath(target, ROOT)))
        else:
            target = os.path.join(ROOT, route.lstrip('/'))
            if not os.path.isfile(target):
                errors.append('[%s] route 檔案不存在：%s' % (section, route))
            elif route.lstrip('/').startswith('public/'):
                with open(target) as fp:
                    head = fp.read(4000)
                if 'auth_check.php' not in head:
                    errors.append('[%s] 獨立頁面 %s 沒有 require core/auth_check.php，任何人都能直接開啟' % (section, route))
        group = get(section, 'group')
        if group:
            used_groups.add(group)
            if group not in groups:
                warnings.append('[%s] group "%s" 沒有對應的 [group.%s]，將以代號當分類標題' % (section, group, group))
        for opt in parser.options(section):
            if opt.startswith('action.'):
                for r in split_list(parser.get(section, opt)):
                    if r not in roles:
                        errors.append('[%s] %s 的身份 %s 未定義' % (section, opt, r))

    for g in sorted(groups - used_groups):
        warnings.append('[group.%s] 沒有任何頁面使用' % g)

    # 身份 → 頁面
    access = {}
    superusers = []
    if not parser.has_section('access'):
        errors.append('缺少 [access] 區段')
    else:
        for role in parser.options('access'):
            if role not in roles:
                errors.append('[access] 身份 %s 未定義於 [roles]' % role)
            items = split_list(parser.get('access', role))
            if '*' in items:
                superusers.append(role)
                access[role] = sorted(pages.keys())
                continue
            for p in items:
                if p not in pages:
                    errors.append('[access] %s 的頁面 %s 沒有對應的 [page.%s]' % (role, p, p))
            access[role] = [p for p in items if p in pages]
    for role in roles:
        if role not in access:
            warnings.append('身份 %s 沒有寫在 [access]，登入後看不到任何頁面' % role)

    for key in sorted(pages):
        if not any(key in access[r] for r in access if r not in superusers):
            warnings.append('頁面 %s 只有 * 身份能使用（若是刻意的可忽略）' % key)

    module_dirs = set(r[len('module:'):].strip() for r in pages.values() if r.startswith('module:'))
    for name in sorted(os.listdir(MODULES)):
        if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(MODULES, name)) and not name.startswith('_') and name not in module_dirs:
            warnings.append('modules/%s 沒有登記在 config.ini，目前無法存取' % name)

    # 圖表
    names = chart_names()
    lower = {}
    for n in names:
        if n.lower() in lower:
            errors.append('charts/%s.ini 與 charts/%s.ini 只差大小寫，頁面代號會衝突' % (lower[n.lower()], n))
        lower[n.lower()] = n
    chart_pages = [k for k, r in pages.items() if r == 'module:charts']
    for key in sorted(chart_pages):
        if key not in lower:
            errors.append('[page.%s] route = module:charts，但 charts/ 裡沒有同名（不分大小寫）的 ini' % key)
        else:
            check_chart(lower[key], errors, warnings)
    for n in names:
        if n.lower() not in chart_pages:
            warnings.append('charts/%s.ini 沒有對應的 [page.%s]（route = "module:charts"），目前無法存取' % (n, n.lower()))

    # 註冊
    if parser.has_section('register'):
        for key in ('listed_role', 'unlisted_role'):
            role = get('register', key)
            if key == 'unlisted_role' and role == '':
                continue
            if role not in roles:
                errors.append('[register] %s "%s" 未定義於 [roles]' % (key, role))
            elif role in superusers:
                errors.append('[register] %s 不可設為超級身份 %s' % (key, role))
    seen = set()
    for account in read_array('ee_accounts', 'account'):
        if not RE_ACCOUNT.match(account):
            errors.append('[ee_accounts] 帳號格式錯誤：%s' % account)
        if account.lower() in seen:
            warnings.append('[ee_accounts] 帳號重複：%s' % account)
        seen.add(account.lower())

    if 'tool_board' in pages:
        check_tool_board(seen, errors, warnings)

    print('== 身份可用頁面 ==')
    for role in roles:
        note = ' (*)' if role in superusers else ''
        print('  %-6s%s: %s' % (role, note, ', '.join(access.get(role, [])) or '-'))
    print('== EE 名單 (%d) ==' % len(seen))
    print('  ' + (', '.join(sorted(seen)) or '-'))
    print()
    for w in warnings:
        print(YELLOW + '[WARN]  ' + w + RESET)
    for e in errors:
        print(RED + '[ERROR] ' + e + RESET)
    if not errors:
        print(GREEN + '[OK] config.ini 檢查通過' + RESET)
    return 1 if errors else 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
#@@@ FILE sql/migrations/20261111_tool_board.sql sha1=8c57ddd8415bde685191dd2147e5850eb3f8a8f3 base=- mode=644 enc=text nl=1
-- =====================================================================
-- 機台借機與 PM 排程看板（modules/tool_board）
-- 伺服器手動執行一次：mysql -u <帳號> -p aisle < sql/migrations/20261111_tool_board.sql
-- 全部是 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS，重複執行不會影響既有資料
-- 機台清單、類別、WIP 處理方式都在 config/tool_board.ini 與 config/tool_board/<機型>.ini，不建表
-- =====================================================================

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Action 主表
--   model / tool_id : 機型代碼（PAN / PAP / POV）與機台
--   category        : 類別代碼（[category.<代碼>]）
--   wip_mode        : WIP 處理方式代碼（該機型檔的 [wip.<代碼>]）
--   start_at/end_at : Action 本體的起訖
--   occupied_end    : end_at + Monitor 時數 + Pilot 時數（尾巴結束），重疊檢查與查詢用
--   version         : 樂觀鎖。每次修改 +1，存檔時帶回讀取當下的 version，不一致回 409
--   刪除採軟刪除（deleted_at）
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `tool_board_actions` (
  `id`            INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `model`         VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL COMMENT '機型代碼',
  `tool_id`       VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL COMMENT '機台',
  `category`      VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL COMMENT 'Action 類別代碼',
  `wip_mode`      VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL COMMENT 'WIP 處理方式代碼',
  `start_at`      DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  `end_at`        DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  `need_monitor`  TINYINT(1)    NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `monitor_hours` DECIMAL(5,1)  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `need_pilot`    TINYINT(1)    NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `pilot_hours`   DECIMAL(5,1)  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `occupied_end`  DATETIME      NOT NULL COMMENT '含 Monitor / Pilot 尾巴的結束時間',
  `remark`        VARCHAR(500)  NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `pm_type`       VARCHAR(64)   NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '由 PM 射程建立時的 PM 類型',
  `version`       INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 COMMENT '樂觀鎖版本',
  `created_by`    INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_by`    INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_by`    INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at`    DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  -- 由程式寫入，輪詢同步依此判斷變動
  `updated_at`    DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  `deleted_at`    DATETIME      NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_tba_tool_time` (`model`, `tool_id`, `start_at`, `occupied_end`),
  KEY `idx_tba_window` (`model`, `deleted_at`, `start_at`),
  KEY `idx_tba_updated` (`model`, `updated_at`),
  CONSTRAINT `fk_tba_created_by` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `fk_tba_updated_by` FOREIGN KEY (`updated_by`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `fk_tba_deleted_by` FOREIGN KEY (`deleted_by`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='借機 / PM 排程看板 Action';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 機台鎖定列：存檔時先 SELECT ... FOR UPDATE 這一列再檢查重疊，
-- 兩個人同時把不同 Action 排進同一台的同一段時間時，後到的會等前一個交易完成再檢查
-- 第一次寫入某台機台時自動建立
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `tool_board_locks` (
  `model`   VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
  `tool_id` VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`model`, `tool_id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='借機看板機台鎖定列';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 異動紀錄：新增 / 修改 / 拖曳 / 刪除，detail 是改前改後（JSON）
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `tool_board_action_log` (
  `id`         INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `action_id`  INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL,
  `user_id`    INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `op`         VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL COMMENT 'create / update / move / delete',
  `detail`     TEXT          NOT NULL,
  `created_at` DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_tbl_action` (`action_id`, `id`),
  CONSTRAINT `fk_tbl_action` FOREIGN KEY (`action_id`)
    REFERENCES `tool_board_actions` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `fk_tbl_user` FOREIGN KEY (`user_id`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='借機看板異動紀錄';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 線上 / 編輯中：每次輪詢更新，超過 presence_seconds 沒更新視為離開
--   action_id 為 NULL = 只是在看；mode = view / edit / drag
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `tool_board_presence` (
  `user_id`   INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL,
  `model`     VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL,
  `action_id` INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `mode`      VARCHAR(8)    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'view',
  `seen_at`   DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`user_id`),
  KEY `idx_tbp_model` (`model`, `seen_at`),
  CONSTRAINT `fk_tbp_user` FOREIGN KEY (`user_id`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='借機看板線上 / 編輯中';
#@@@ FILE sql/schema.sql sha1=730f95b315605373d0f979f74f35fc69be8aeafc base=37a2f3d6a2e9901637bb6a7c8c738862434cb04d mode=644 enc=text nl=1
-- =====================================================================
-- AISLE 資料庫結構 (MariaDB 5.5 / 10.x 皆可)
-- 字元集：utf8mb4；若 MariaDB < 5.5.3 不支援 utf8mb4，請全文取代為 utf8
-- 注意：InnoDB 舊版索引上限 767 bytes，utf8mb4 的 VARCHAR 索引欄位請 <= 191
-- =====================================================================

CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS `aisle`
  DEFAULT CHARACTER SET utf8mb4
  COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

USE `aisle`;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 使用者
--   password_hash : bcrypt 雜湊 ($2y$...，固定 60 字元)，
--                   保留 255 長度以便日後換演算法
--   role          : 對應 config/permissions.ini 的 [roles] (EE / PE / ADMIN)
--                   刻意不用 ENUM，新增角色時只改 INI 不必 ALTER TABLE
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `users` (
  `id`                   INT UNSIGNED      NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `account`              VARCHAR(64)       NOT NULL COMMENT '登入帳號',
  `password_hash`        VARCHAR(255)      NOT NULL COMMENT 'bcrypt 雜湊',
  `display_name`         VARCHAR(100)      NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `email`                VARCHAR(191)      NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `role`                 VARCHAR(16)       NOT NULL DEFAULT 'EE' COMMENT 'EE / PE / ADMIN',
  `is_active`            TINYINT(1)        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  `must_change_password` TINYINT(1)        NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `failed_attempts`      SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 COMMENT '連續登入失敗次數',
  `locked_until`         DATETIME          NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '鎖定到期時間',
  `last_login_at`        DATETIME          NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `last_login_ip`        VARCHAR(45)       NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '可容納 IPv6',
  `created_at`           DATETIME          NOT NULL,
  -- MariaDB 5.5 一張表只能有一個 TIMESTAMP 使用 CURRENT_TIMESTAMP，因此 created_at 用 DATETIME 由程式寫入
  `updated_at`           TIMESTAMP         NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `uk_users_account` (`account`),
  KEY `idx_users_role` (`role`),
  KEY `idx_users_active` (`is_active`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='系統使用者';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 登入紀錄 (成功與失敗都記錄，供稽核與暴力破解偵測)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `login_logs` (
  `id`          BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `user_id`     INT UNSIGNED    NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '帳號不存在時為 NULL',
  `account`     VARCHAR(64)     NOT NULL,
  `success`     TINYINT(1)      NOT NULL,
  `reason`      VARCHAR(32)     NOT NULL DEFAULT '' COMMENT 'ok / bad_password / no_user / locked / inactive',
  `ip`          VARCHAR(45)     NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `user_agent`  VARCHAR(255)    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `created_at`  DATETIME        NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_login_logs_user` (`user_id`, `created_at`),
  KEY `idx_login_logs_ip` (`ip`, `created_at`),
  CONSTRAINT `fk_login_logs_user` FOREIGN KEY (`user_id`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='登入紀錄';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 操作稽核 (各模組寫入，例如：匯出報表、修改設定)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `audit_logs` (
  `id`          BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `user_id`     INT UNSIGNED    NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `module`      VARCHAR(64)     NOT NULL,
  `action`      VARCHAR(64)     NOT NULL,
  `detail`      TEXT            NULL COMMENT 'JSON 字串',
  `ip`          VARCHAR(45)     NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `created_at`  DATETIME        NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_audit_module` (`module`, `action`, `created_at`),
  KEY `idx_audit_user` (`user_id`, `created_at`),
  CONSTRAINT `fk_audit_logs_user` FOREIGN KEY (`user_id`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='操作稽核';

-- =====================================================================
-- Pending Issue Control Table（與 sql/migrations/20261110_pending_issue.sql 相同）
-- =====================================================================
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Issue 主表
--   model     : 機型代碼（config/pending_issue.ini 的 [models]，例 PAN / PAP / POV）
--   category  : 分類代碼（[categories]，例 hl / tool_select / aging_parts / others）
--   status    : open / monitor / close（[status]）
--   sponsor_id: AISLE 使用者
--   version   : 樂觀鎖。每次修改 +1，存檔時帶回讀取當下的 version，不一致回 409
--   刪除採軟刪除（deleted_at），只有 ADMIN 能看到與還原
--   代碼刻意不用 ENUM：新增機型 / 分類只改 ini，不必 ALTER TABLE
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `pending_issues` (
  `id`                INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `tool_id`           VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL COMMENT '機台代號',
  `model`             VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL COMMENT '機型代碼',
  `category`          VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL COMMENT '分類代碼',
  `issue`             TEXT          NOT NULL COMMENT 'Issue 描述',
  `initial_date`      DATE          NOT NULL,
  `status`            VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' COMMENT 'open / monitor / close',
  `status_changed_by` INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '最後改狀態的使用者',
  `status_changed_at` DATETIME      NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `sponsor_id`        INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL COMMENT 'Sponsor（AISLE 使用者）',
  `checkpoint_date`   DATE          NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `remark`            TEXT          NULL,
  `closed_at`         DATETIME      NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `version`           INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 COMMENT '樂觀鎖版本',
  `created_by`        INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_by`        INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_by`        INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at`        DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  -- 由程式寫入（MariaDB 5.5 一張表只能有一個自動更新的 TIMESTAMP），輪詢同步依此判斷變動
  `updated_at`        DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  `deleted_at`        DATETIME      NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_pi_filter` (`deleted_at`, `model`, `category`, `status`),
  KEY `idx_pi_tool` (`tool_id`),
  KEY `idx_pi_sponsor` (`sponsor_id`),
  KEY `idx_pi_updated` (`updated_at`, `id`),
  KEY `idx_pi_checkpoint` (`checkpoint_date`),
  CONSTRAINT `fk_pi_sponsor` FOREIGN KEY (`sponsor_id`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `fk_pi_status_by` FOREIGN KEY (`status_changed_by`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `fk_pi_created_by` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `fk_pi_updated_by` FOREIGN KEY (`updated_by`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `fk_pi_deleted_by` FOREIGN KEY (`deleted_by`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='Pending Issue Control Table';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 狀態變更紀錄：新增時記 NULL → open，之後每次狀態改變記一筆（誰、何時）
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `pending_issue_status_logs` (
  `id`           INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `issue_id`     INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL,
  `from_status`  VARCHAR(16)   NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `to_status`    VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL,
  `changed_by`   INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `changed_at`   DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_pisl_issue` (`issue_id`, `changed_at`),
  CONSTRAINT `fk_pisl_issue` FOREIGN KEY (`issue_id`)
    REFERENCES `pending_issues` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `fk_pisl_user` FOREIGN KEY (`changed_by`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='Pending Issue 狀態變更紀錄';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Issue 圖片：檔案存在 storage/uploads/pending_issue/<年月>/，這裡只記路徑
--   貼上 / 拖曳 / 選檔時先上傳（issue_id 為 NULL），按儲存才綁定到 issue；
--   超過一天仍未綁定的暫存圖，會在下次有人上傳時清除
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `pending_issue_images` (
  `id`            INT UNSIGNED      NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `issue_id`      INT UNSIGNED      NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `file_path`     VARCHAR(191)      NOT NULL COMMENT '相對於 storage/uploads/',
  `original_name` VARCHAR(191)      NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `mime`          VARCHAR(32)       NOT NULL,
  `size`          INT UNSIGNED      NOT NULL,
  `width`         SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `height`        SMALLINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `uploaded_by`   INT UNSIGNED      NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at`    DATETIME          NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_pii_issue` (`issue_id`, `created_at`),
  CONSTRAINT `fk_pii_issue` FOREIGN KEY (`issue_id`)
    REFERENCES `pending_issues` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `fk_pii_user` FOREIGN KEY (`uploaded_by`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='Pending Issue 圖片';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 誰正在編輯哪一筆（編輯視窗開著時隨輪詢更新，超過 presence_seconds 沒更新視為離開）
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `pending_issue_presence` (
  `issue_id`  INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL,
  `user_id`   INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL,
  `seen_at`   DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`issue_id`, `user_id`),
  KEY `idx_pip_user` (`user_id`),
  KEY `idx_pip_seen` (`seen_at`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='Pending Issue 編輯中狀態';

-- =====================================================================
-- 機台借機與 PM 排程看板（與 sql/migrations/20261111_tool_board.sql 相同）
-- =====================================================================

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Action 主表
--   model / tool_id : 機型代碼（PAN / PAP / POV）與機台
--   category        : 類別代碼（[category.<代碼>]）
--   wip_mode        : WIP 處理方式代碼（該機型檔的 [wip.<代碼>]）
--   start_at/end_at : Action 本體的起訖
--   occupied_end    : end_at + Monitor 時數 + Pilot 時數（尾巴結束），重疊檢查與查詢用
--   version         : 樂觀鎖。每次修改 +1，存檔時帶回讀取當下的 version，不一致回 409
--   刪除採軟刪除（deleted_at）
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `tool_board_actions` (
  `id`            INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `model`         VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL COMMENT '機型代碼',
  `tool_id`       VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL COMMENT '機台',
  `category`      VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL COMMENT 'Action 類別代碼',
  `wip_mode`      VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL COMMENT 'WIP 處理方式代碼',
  `start_at`      DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  `end_at`        DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  `need_monitor`  TINYINT(1)    NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `monitor_hours` DECIMAL(5,1)  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `need_pilot`    TINYINT(1)    NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  `pilot_hours`   DECIMAL(5,1)  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `occupied_end`  DATETIME      NOT NULL COMMENT '含 Monitor / Pilot 尾巴的結束時間',
  `remark`        VARCHAR(500)  NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  `pm_type`       VARCHAR(64)   NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '由 PM 射程建立時的 PM 類型',
  `version`       INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 COMMENT '樂觀鎖版本',
  `created_by`    INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `updated_by`    INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `deleted_by`    INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `created_at`    DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  -- 由程式寫入，輪詢同步依此判斷變動
  `updated_at`    DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  `deleted_at`    DATETIME      NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_tba_tool_time` (`model`, `tool_id`, `start_at`, `occupied_end`),
  KEY `idx_tba_window` (`model`, `deleted_at`, `start_at`),
  KEY `idx_tba_updated` (`model`, `updated_at`),
  CONSTRAINT `fk_tba_created_by` FOREIGN KEY (`created_by`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `fk_tba_updated_by` FOREIGN KEY (`updated_by`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `fk_tba_deleted_by` FOREIGN KEY (`deleted_by`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='借機 / PM 排程看板 Action';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 機台鎖定列：存檔時先 SELECT ... FOR UPDATE 這一列再檢查重疊，
-- 兩個人同時把不同 Action 排進同一台的同一段時間時，後到的會等前一個交易完成再檢查
-- 第一次寫入某台機台時自動建立
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `tool_board_locks` (
  `model`   VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
  `tool_id` VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`model`, `tool_id`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='借機看板機台鎖定列';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 異動紀錄：新增 / 修改 / 拖曳 / 刪除，detail 是改前改後（JSON）
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `tool_board_action_log` (
  `id`         INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `action_id`  INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL,
  `user_id`    INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `op`         VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL COMMENT 'create / update / move / delete',
  `detail`     TEXT          NOT NULL,
  `created_at` DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `idx_tbl_action` (`action_id`, `id`),
  CONSTRAINT `fk_tbl_action` FOREIGN KEY (`action_id`)
    REFERENCES `tool_board_actions` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE,
  CONSTRAINT `fk_tbl_user` FOREIGN KEY (`user_id`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE SET NULL ON UPDATE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='借機看板異動紀錄';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 線上 / 編輯中：每次輪詢更新，超過 presence_seconds 沒更新視為離開
--   action_id 為 NULL = 只是在看；mode = view / edit / drag
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `tool_board_presence` (
  `user_id`   INT UNSIGNED  NOT NULL,
  `model`     VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL,
  `action_id` INT UNSIGNED  NULL DEFAULT NULL,
  `mode`      VARCHAR(8)    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'view',
  `seen_at`   DATETIME      NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (`user_id`),
  KEY `idx_tbp_model` (`model`, `seen_at`),
  CONSTRAINT `fk_tbp_user` FOREIGN KEY (`user_id`)
    REFERENCES `users` (`id`) ON DELETE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci COMMENT='借機看板線上 / 編輯中';
